# Earth Fauna — 631 One-by-One Anatomy Review

Status definitions:

- **PASS:** leave alone at the current stylized resolution.
- **HOLD:** broadly recognizable/correctly routed, but not specific enough for final real-world accuracy approval.
- **FAIL:** clear anatomical, growth-form, morphology, or readability correction required.

## 1. Aardvark — FAIL

**Expected family/type:** Unusual African mammals
**Changed from previous submission:** Yes

- **Overall body plan:** FAIL
- **Torso:** FAIL
- **Head:** FAIL
- **Eyes:** PASS
- **Legs/wings/fins/appendages:** FAIL
- **Rear/tail/flukes:** FAIL
- **Signature traits:** FAIL
- **Global-template risk:** HIGH

**Assessment:** The current animal is still a generic hoofed/rabbit-like quadruped. Rebuild the torso with an arched back and low shoulders; lengthen the tubular snout; enlarge the ears; add heavy digging forelimbs with claws; and replace the thin tail with a thick tapering aardvark tail.

- **Signature traits:** FAIL

**Assessment:** The full silhouette is not equine. The torso is a flat rectangle rather than a deep barrel with withers; the head is short and rounded instead of a long wedge-shaped horse skull; the neck rises too vertically and lacks a mane; all four legs are straight rods without knees, hocks, fetlocks, pasterns or hooves; the hindquarters are square and under-muscled; and the tail is a short radial tuft. Build a dedicated equid scaffold with sloped shoulders, deep chest, muscular croup, articulated limbs, real hooves, mane and a long flowing tail.
